    CHAIRMAN’S LETTER
    董事長信札


    To My Fellow Shareholders,                                                 致我的股東同仁：

    2025 is the start of our second 25-Year. It also represents the start of   二零二五年是我們第二個 25 年的開始。這也
    our second startup. There was both joy and grief in this remarkable        代表我們第二次創業的開始。這非凡的一年
    year.                                                                      既有喜悅也有憂慮。

    Our net profit for the Year was approximately RMB2.5 billion or            我們本年度純利為約人民幣 2,500,000,000 元，
    RMB2.3 billion excluding the compensation from Meihua. Although            或 人 民 幣 2,300,000,000 元（不 包 括 梅 花 的 賠
    the selling prices of our key products continued to decline over the       償）。儘管我們主要產品的售價在全年持續下
    year, the increase in sales volume and the decrease in the costs of        跌，但銷售量的增加及主要原料成本的下降，
    major raw materials helped maintain the net profit at a similar level to   幫助純利維持在與去年相似的水平。
    last year.

    A big step in our internationalisation is encouraging. After years of      我們國際化邁出一大步令人鼓舞。經過多年
    unremitting efforts, we started the construction of phase I of our first   不懈努力，我們於本年度開始在哈薩克斯坦
    overseas production base in Kazakhstan in the Year. The target is to       建設首個海外生產基地的第一期工程。目標
    commence the production and contribute to our results in 2026.             是在二零二六年開始生產並為我們的業績作
                                                                               出貢獻。

    2026 may be another extraordinary year posing a big challenge for          二零二六年或許又是一個不尋常的一年，對
    us.                                                                        我們來說是一大挑戰。

    Since 2024, the selling prices of some of our products have begun          自二零二四年起，我們部分產品的售價開始
    to decline. The decline in selling prices spread to all products in 2025   下 降。從 二 零 二 五 年 起，售 價 的 下 降 蔓 延 至
    and continued until early 2026, which may affect the results for 2026.     所 有 產 品，並 持 續 至 二 零 二 六 年 初，這 可 能
    At the time of writing this letter, the selling prices of all products     會影響二零二六年的業績。撰寫此信札時，
    increased. This may indicate that the price cycle reached its bottom       所有產品的售價均已提升。這可能表示價格
    in early 2026 and entered the rising phase.                                週期於二零二六年初觸底並進入上升階段。

    Although the price cycle may be entering a rising phase, we should         儘管價格週期可能正進入上升階段，我們仍
    remain alert to the potential impact of ongoing geopolitical tensions      應保持警覺，關注持續地緣政治緊張對全球
    on the global economy.                                                     經濟可能產生的影響。

    Although there has been an advancement in our internationalisation         儘 管 如 前 所 述，我 們 的 國 際 化 有 所 進 展，但
    as mentioned above, there is a running-in period for the Kazakhstan        哈薩克斯坦的生產基地仍有一段磨合期。這
    production base. It may take time before contributing to our               可能需要時間才能為我們的盈利能力作出貢獻。
    profitability.

    All in all, using my words in the 2024 annual report, I wish Fufeng        總 之，借 用 我 在 二 零 二 四 年 年 報 中 的 話，我
    become a multinational enterprise in this second 25-Year.                  希望阜豐在這第二個 25 年成為跨國企業。

    The global economy faced uncertainties and chaos driven by the U.S.       全球經濟面臨美國關稅政策及地緣政治風險
    tariff policies and geopolitical risks. The International Monetary Fund   所帶來的不確定因素與混亂局面。國際貨幣
    projected the global economic growth at 3.2% for 2025. China’s            基金會預測二零二五年全球經濟增長 3.2%。
    economy, as reported by the National Bureau of Statistics of China,       據中國國家統計局的報告顯示，二零二五年
    grew by 5.0% in 2025.                                                     中國經濟增長 5.0%。

Business and Financial Review                                                      業務及財務回顧
Overall Performance                                                                整體表現
With a diversified product development plan, the business structure                本集團通過多元化產品開發規劃，建立了多
of multiple growth drivers has been established, enhancing the core                個增長動力的業務結構，提升本集團的核心
competitiveness of the Group.                                                      競爭力。

Facing the challenging market situation, the Group maintained stable               面對充滿挑戰的市場形勢，本集團憑藉多元
revenue leveraging on a diversified product portfolio.                             化產品組合，仍能保持穩定的收入。

Corn price remained low for the Year compared to last year. The ASP                與去年相比，本年度玉米價格維持在低位。
continued to decrease throughout the Year. The impact of the decline               全年平均售價持續下跌。本年度上半年玉米
in corn price in the first half of the Year outweighed the impact of the           價格下跌的影響遠超平均售價下降的影響，
decrease in ASP which led to the good performance of the Group.                    從而使本集團表現良好。本年度下半年平均
The effect of the continuous decrease in the ASP in the second half                售價持續下跌的影響大於玉米價格低的影響，
of the Year was greater than the impact of the low corn price which                導致業績遜於本年度上半年。
led to worsened performance than the first half of the Year.

The Group’s revenue slightly increased by 0.4% to approximately                    於本年度，本集團的收入較二零二四年微增
RMB27,879.2 million in the Year, as compared to 2024, mainly due                   0.4% 至 約 人 民 幣 27,879,200,000 元，主 要 由
to the increase in revenue of the animal nutrition segment.                        於動物營養分部的收入增加所致。

The Group’s gross profit increased by 8.4% to approximately                        於本年度，本集團的毛利較二零二四年增加
RMB5,481.9 million in the Year as compared to 2024, primarily due                  8.4% 至約人民幣 5,481,900,000 元，主要由於
to the increased gross profit contribution from the food additives and             食品添加劑及動物營養分部毛利貢獻增加所致。
animal nutrition segments.

approximately RMB2,465.2 million in 2025, as compared to 2024.                     6.6% 至約人民幣 2,465,200,000 元。

     Uyghur Forced Labour Prevention Act                                       《防止強迫維吾爾人勞動法》
     Based on the Uyghur Forced Labour Prevention Act implemented               基 於 美 國 於 二 零 二 二 年 六 月 執 行《防 止 強 迫
     by the U.S. in June 2022, the U.S. Customs and Border Protection           維吾爾人勞動法》後，美國海關與邊境保護局
     (“CBP”) requires non-Xinjiang-related and non-forced-labour               （「CBP」）要求提交非涉疆和非涉及強迫勞動
     supporting materials to be submitted and approved for clearance of         的證明資料並經批准，以便本集團的出口產
     the Group’s exported products into the U.S. market.                        品通關進入美國市場。

     The Group confirmed that no “forced labour” is used in our                本集團確認，我們的生產中並無使用「強迫勞
     production. The Group provides supporting materials of the exported       動」。本集團向 CBP 提供出口產品的證明資料
     products to CBP for approval and then the products are continuously       以供批准，其後產品不斷通關並進入美國市場。
     cleared and released into the U.S. market.

     Executive Summary                                                       管理層摘要
     As a leading bio-fermentation company, we deliver positive impacts      作為一家領先的生物發酵公司，我們致力於
     and create value for our stakeholders. We achieve this goal by          為利益相關者創造積極影響和價值。我們通
     advancing environmental sustainability, supporting our customers,       過推進環境可持續發展、支持客戶、賦能員工、
     empowering our people, building strong partnerships and fostering       建立牢固的合作夥伴關係，以及促進社會繁
     thriving communities.                                                   榮來實現這一目標。

     We adhere to green development, with a core focus on the circular       我 們 堅 持 綠 色 發 展，以 循 環 經 濟 為 核 心，全
     economy, and are committed to building a green, low-carbon, and         力打造綠色、低碳、循環的產業鏈。
     circular industrial chain.

The four key steps in the risk management process are:                      風險管理程序中的四個關鍵步驟如下：

•      Risk identification and assessment – identify the key risks          •   風險識別及評估－識別本集團的主要風
       of the Group and analyse the risk by considering the possibility         險，並透過考慮事件發生的可能性及對
       of occurrence and the impact to the Group;                               本集團的影響分析有關風險；

•      Risk handling – adopt an appropriate risk management                 •   風險處理－就每項關鍵風險採取適當的
       strategy (i.e. risk response) for each key risk;                         風險管理策略（即風險回應）；

•      Risk monitoring – apply monitoring mechanism to ensure               •   風 險 監 察－採 用 監 察 機 制，確 保 風 險 回
       the risk response are operated effectively;                              應行之有效；

•      Risk reporting – summarise the result of risk assessment             •   風 險 匯 報－概 述 風 險 評 估 結 果，並 向 風
       and report to the Risk Management Team.                                  險管理小組匯報。

In the risk management process, the top risks within the Group              在風險管理過程中會識別及評估本集團內部
are identified and assessed; and the respective risk management             的 首 要 風 險，隨 後 管 理 層 會 討 論、協 定 及 實
measures as well as the corresponding mitigating controls are               施相關風險管理措施和相應的紓緩控制，並
discussed, agreed and implemented by the management. Risk                   由風險管理小組每年向董事會及審計委員會
assessment results are reported by the Risk Management Team to              匯報有關風險評估結果。
the Board and Audit Committee annually.

Principal Risks and Uncertainty                                           主要風險及不確定因素
The principal risks and uncertainties of the Group are highlighted        本集團的主要風險及不確定因素如下所示：
below:

Stable Supply of Raw Materials                                            原材料穩定供應
The major raw material of the Group is corn. Being an agricultural        本集團的主要原材料為玉米。作為農產品，
product, the plantation and supply of corn may be affected by the         玉米的種植和供應可能會受到天氣或意外事
weather or unexpected events. If the stable supply of corn is affected    件的影響。倘玉米的穩定供應受到惡劣天氣
by bad weather or unexpected events, the price of corn, which in turn     或意外事件的影響，玉米價格可能會上漲，
our cost of production, may increase.                                     進而增加我們的生產成本。

Geopolitics                                                               地緣政治
The Group is expanding through internationalisation, which involves       本集團正通過國際化進行擴張，其中包括在
operations in countries outside the PRC. The policies of these            中國以外的國家開展業務。該等國家的政策
countries may affect the operations of the Group.                         可能會影響本集團的運營。

diversified products. The objectives of the Group are to maximise the     本集團的目標是實現溢利最大化及向股東分
profits and to distribute dividends to Shareholders. Hence, the Key       派 股 息。因 此，關 鍵 績 效 指 標 是 股 東 應 佔 溢
performance indicators are the profits attributable to the Shareholders   利和股息支付。
and dividend payout.

     Dividend Policy                                                            股息政策
     The Board may declare dividend from time to time. The Board has            董事會可不時宣派股息。董事會於考慮以下
     absolute discretion in the declaration of dividend after taking into       因素後全權酌情決定股息之宣派：
     account the following factors:

     1.    Reserves of the Group available for distribution;                    1.   本集團可供分派之儲備；
     2.    Performance of the Group;                                            2.   本集團之表現；
     3.    Anticipation of future outlook of the economy;                       3.   預測未來經濟前景；

     BASIS FOR OPINION                                                           意見的基礎
     We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards               我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的《香港審計

     section of our report.                                                      闡述。

     We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and       我們相信，我們所獲得的審計憑證能充足及

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the        我們的目標，是對合併財務報表整體是否不

towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this   我們不會就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負
report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not      上或承擔任何責任。合理保證是高水平的保
a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will          證，但不能保證按照《香港審計準則》進行的
always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements        審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。
can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,              錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理
individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to     預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響合併財務

consolidated financial statements.                                         經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。

1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                               1. 一般資料
  Fufeng Group Limited and its subsidiaries manufacture and sell       阜豐集團有限公司及其附屬公司從事製
  fermentation-based food additives, biochemical products and          造及銷售發酵食品添加劑、生化產品及
  starch-based products. The Group has manufacturing plants in         澱粉產品。本集團於中國山東省、陝西省、
  Shandong Province, Shaanxi Province, Heilongjiang Province,          黑龍江省、內蒙古自治區及新疆維吾爾
  Inner Mongolia Autonomous Region and Xinjiang Uygur                  自治區設有生產廠房，主要向中國客戶
  Autonomous Region of the PRC and sells mainly to customers           進行銷售。
  located in the PRC.

  The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in           本公司為於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公
  the Cayman Islands. The address of its registered office is          司，其註冊辦事處地址為 Cricket Square,
  Cricket Square, Hutchins Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand Cayman          Hutchins Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand
  KY1-1111, Cayman Islands.                                            Cayman KY1-1111, Cayman Islands。

  The Company has its shares listed on the Stock Exchange.             本公司股份在聯交所上市。

  unless otherwise stated. These consolidated financial statements     民幣列示。此等合併財務報表已於二零

     3. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (Continued)                                 3. 財務風險管理（續）
        3.2 Capital management                                                  3.2 資本管理
            (a)   Risk management                                                 (a)   風險管理
                  The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to:                  本集團的資本管理目標為：

                  •    safeguard their ability to continue as a going                   •   保障其按持續經營基準繼
                       concern, so that they can continue to provide                        續營運的能力，因此彼等
                       returns for shareholders and benefits for other                      可繼續為股東帶來回報，
                       stakeholders, and                                                    同時兼顧其他持份者的利
                                                                                            益，及

                  •    maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce                  •   維持最佳資本結構以減低
                       the cost of capital.                                                 資金成本。

                  In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure,                 為維持或調整資本架構，本集
                  the Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid                     團或會調整支付予股東的股息
                  to shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue                金 額、向 股 東 發 還 資 本、發 行
                  new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                             新股或出售資產以減低債務。

                  The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing                本集團根據資產負債比率監
                  ratio. This ratio is equal to total debt divided by total             察其資本。該比率相等於債務
                  assets at the end of corresponding year. Total debt                   總額除以對應年度年結日資
                  includes current and non-current bank borrowings as                   產總值。於二零二五年十二月
                  at 31 December 2025.                                                  三十一日，債務總額包括流動
                                                                                        及非流動銀行借貸。

                        The Group’s policy is to recognise transfers into                     本集團政策旨在確認於報
                        and transfers out of fair value hierarchy levels as                   告期末公平值等級水平的
                        at the end of the reporting period.                                   轉入及轉出情況。

                        Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments                      第 1 層 級：在 活 躍 市 場 上
                        traded in active markets (such as publicly traded                     買 賣 的 金 融 工 具（如 公 開
                        derivatives, and equity securities) is based on                       買賣衍生工具及股本證券）
                        quoted market prices at the end of the reporting                      的公平值乃按於報告期末
                        period. The quoted market price used for                              的市場報價釐定。本集團
                        financial assets held by the Group is the current                     所持有金融資產採用的市
                        bid price. These instruments are included in                          場報價為當時買盤價。該
                        level 1.                                                              等工具計入第 1 層級。

                        Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments                      第 2 層 級：未 在 活 躍 市 場
                        that are not traded in an active market (for                          上 買 賣 的 金 融 工 具（如 場
                        example, over-the-counter derivatives) is                             外 衍 生 工 具）的 公 平 值 採
                        determined using valuation techniques which                           用估值技術釐定。該等估
                        maximise the use of observable market data                            值技術盡量採用可觀察市
                        and rely as little as possible on entity-specific                     場數據，並盡可能不倚賴
                        estimates. If all significant inputs required to                      實體特定估計。倘計算工
                        fair value an instrument are observable, the                          具公平值所需的所有重大
                        instrument is included in level 2.                                    輸入數據均可觀察，則該
                                                                                              工具計入第 2 層級。

                        Level 3: If one or more of the significant inputs                     第 3 層 級：倘 一 項 或 多 項
                        is not based on observable market data, the                           重大輸入數據並非以可觀
                        instrument is included in level 3. This is the case                   察市場數據為基礎，則該
                        for unlisted equity securities and for instruments                    工 具 計 入 第 3 層 級。對 於
                        where climate risk gives rise to a significant                        非上市股本證券及氣候風
                        unobservable adjustment.                                              險會導致重大不可觀察調
                                                                                              整的工具而言便是如此。

4. CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                       4. 重要會計估計及判斷
   JUDGEMENTS
   The preparation of financial statements requires the use of               財務報表之編製需要使用會計估計，根
   accounting estimates which, by definition, will likely differ from        據定義，該等會計估計很可能有別於實
   actual results. Management also needs to exercise judgment in             際結果。管理層亦需在應用本集團的會
   applying the Group’s accounting policies.                                 計政策時作出判斷。

   Estimates and judgments are continually evaluated and are                 對估計及判斷作出持續評估，並以過往
   based on historical experience and other factors, including               經驗及其他因素作基礎，包括在目前情
   expectations of future events that are believed to be reasonable          況下對未來事件的合理預測。
   under the circumstances.

   The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the                  本集團對未來作出估計及假設。所得會
   future. The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition,           計估計一如其定義甚少與相關的實際結
   seldom equal the related actual results. The estimates and                果相同。以下所述的估計及假設有相當
   assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material            大風險會引致須於下一個財政年度對資
   adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities              產及負債的賬面值作出重大調整。
   within the next financial year are described below.

              Accounting policies of revenue recognition                                 收益確認的會計政策
              Sales of goods                                                             銷售貨品
              The Group manufactures and sells a range of fermentation-                  本集團在全球市場製造及銷售一系
              based food additives, biochemical products and starch-                     列發酵食品添加劑、生化產品及澱
              based products in the worldwide market. Sales are                          粉 產 品。當 產 品 控 制 權 已 轉 讓（即
              recognised when control of the products has transferred,                   產 品 交 付 予 客 戶 之 時）、客 戶 對 銷
              being when the products are delivered to the customer,                     售產品的渠道及價格有完全決定權、
              the customer has full discretion over the channel and price                且不存在可能影響客戶接收產品的
              to sell the products, and there is no unfulfilled obligation               未 履 行 義 務 時，確 認 銷 售。當 產 品
              that could affect the customer’s acceptance of the                         交付到指定地點或由客戶指定的承
              products. Delivery occurs when the products have been                      運人收貨（就境內銷售而言）或已裝
              delivered to the specified location or have been picked up                 船（就海外銷售而言），產品毀損及
              by carriers designated by customers for domestic sales,                    損失的風險已轉移給客戶，及客戶
              or have been shipped on board for overseas sales, the                      已根據銷售合約接納產品，接納條
              risks of obsolescence and loss have been transferred                       文已失效，或本集團有客觀證據證
              to the customer, and either the customer has accepted                      明已達成接納之所有條件時，即為
              the products in accordance with the sales contract, the                    交付。
              acceptance provisions have lapsed, or the Group has

              satisfied.

  (d) Impairment loss                                                    (d) 減值虧損
       Management judgment is required in the area of asset                 管理層須判斷資產減值範疇，尤其
       impairment particularly in assessing: (i) whether an event           是 評 估：(i) 是 否 已 發 生 可 能 顯 示 有
       has occurred that may indicate that the related assets               關 資 產 價 值 或 不 可 收 回 的 事 件；(ii)
       values may not be recoverable; (ii) whether the carrying             可 收 回 金 額（即 公 平 值 減 出 售 成 本
       value of an asset can be supported by the recoverable                或按於業務中持續使用資產估計的
       amount, being the higher of fair value less costs of                 未來現金流量淨現值兩者中的較高
       disposal or net present value of future cash flows which             者）能 否 支 持 該 項 資 產 的 賬 面 值；
       are estimated based upon the continued use of the asset              及 (iii) 於編製現金流量預測中應用的
       in the business; and (iii) the appropriate key assumptions           適當主要假設，包括該等現金流量
       to be applied in preparing cash flow projections including           預測是否使用適當貼現率貼現。倘
       whether these cash flow projections are discounted using             管理層選擇用以評估減值的假設（包
       an appropriate rate. Changing the assumptions selected               括現金流量預測中採用的貼現率或
       by management in assessing impairment, including the                 增 長 率 假 設）改 變，則 可 能 會 對 減
       discount rates or the growth rate assumptions in the cash            值測試中的淨現值產生重大影響，
       flow projections, could materially affect the net present            因而影響本集團的財務狀況及經營
       value in the impairment test and as a result affect the              業績。倘預計表現及相應未來現金
       Group’s financial condition and results of operations. If            流量預測出現重大不利變動，則可
       there is a significant adverse change in the projected               能須在合併利潤表中扣除減值開支。
       performance and resulting future cash flow projections,              倘有跡象顯示減值虧損可能已減少，
       it may be necessary to take an impairment charge to the              則可收回金額不得超過假設並無確
       consolidated income statement. If there is an indication that        認減值的情況下已折舊歷史成本。
       an impairment loss may have decreased, the recoverable
       amount should not be more than what the depreciated
       historical cost would have been if the impairment had not
       been recognised.

  (b) Net realisable value of inventories                              (b) 存貨的可變現淨值
      Net realisable value of inventories is the estimated selling        存貨的可變現淨值乃於日常業務過
      price in the ordinary course of business, less estimated            程中作出的估計售價減估計完成成
      costs of completion and selling expenses. These estimates           本及銷售開支。該等估計根據現時
      are based on the current market condition and historical            市況及製造及出售性質類似產品的
      experience of manufacturing and selling products of similar         過往經驗作出，可能因客戶偏好變
      nature. It could change significantly as a result of changes        化及競爭對手因應行業週期所採取
      in customer taste and competitor actions in response to             的行動而出現重大變動。管理層於
      industry cycles. Management reassesses the estimates at             各報告日期重新評估有關估計。
      each reporting date.

       In accordance with the PRC regulations and the articles              根據中國規例及組成本集團的旗下
       of the association of the PRC companies comprising the               各中國公司的組織章程細則規定，
       Group, before distributing the net profit of each year, each         在分派各年純利前，每間在中國註
       of the companies registered in the PRC is required to                冊的公司均須按中國會計準則規定，
       set aside 10% of its statutory net profit for the year after         於對銷任何上一年度虧損後，將該
       offsetting any prior year’s losses as determined under the           年度的法定純利 10% 撥入法定盈餘
       PRC accounting regulations to the statutory surplus reserve          儲備基金。倘該儲備的結餘達到各
       fund. When the balance of such reserve reaches 50% of                公司股本的 50%，其後可自行決定
       each company’s share capital, any further appropriation is           任何撥調。法定盈餘儲備基金可用
       optional. The statutory surplus reserve fund can be utilised         以對銷過往年度虧損或發行紅股，
       to offset prior years’ losses or to issue bonus shares,              惟有關儲備結餘不少於發行紅股後
       provided that the balance of such reserve is not less than           實體註冊資本的 25%。

    POLICIES INFORMATION
  This note provides a list of other accounting policies adopted          此附註提供一系列編製此等合併財務報
  in the preparation of these consolidated financial statements.          表採納的其他會計政策。除另有註明外，
  These policies have been consistently applied to all the years          此等政策在所列報的所有年度內貫徹應
  presented, unless otherwise stated. The financial statements            用。財務報表適用於包含阜豐集團有限
  are for the Group consisting of Fufeng Group Limited and its            公司及其附屬公司的本集團。
  subsidiaries.

  39.1 Principles of consolidation and equity                             39.1 合併及權益會計法原則
       accounting
      (i)    Subsidiaries                                                     (i)    附屬公司
             Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                     附屬公司指本集團對其具有控
             entities) over which the Group has control. The Group                   制 權 的 所 有 實 體（包 括 結 構 性
             controls an entity where the Group is exposed to, or                    實 體）。當 本 集 團 因 為 參 與 該
             has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with               實體而承擔可變回報的風險或
             the entity and has the ability to affect those returns                  享有可變回報的權益，並有能
             through its power to direct the activities of the entity.               力透過控制該實體活動的權力
             Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on                    影響此等回報時，本集團即控
             which control is transferred to the Group. They are                     制該實體。附屬公司在控制權
             deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                       轉移至本集團之日起全部合併
                                                                                     入賬。附屬公司在控制權終止
                                                                                     之日起停止合併入賬。

             Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised                     公司間交易、結餘及本集團旗
             gains on transactions between Group companies                           下公司之間的交易未變現利得
             are eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated                   均予以抵銷。未變現虧損亦會
             unless the transaction provides evidence of an                          抵銷，惟有關交易提供證據顯
             impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting                         示所轉讓資產出現減值，則另
             policies of subsidiaries have been changed where                        當別論。附屬公司的會計政策
             necessary to ensure consistency with the policies                       已在需要時作出變更，以確保
             adopted by the Group.                                                   與本集團採用的政策保持一致。

      (ii)   Associates                                                       (ii)   聯營公司
             Associates are all entities over which the Group                        聯營公司指所有本集團對其有
             has significant influence but not control or joint                      重大影響力而無控制或共同控
             control. This is generally the case where the Group                     制權的實體。此種情況通常為
             holds between 20% and 50% of the voting rights.                         本集團持有 20% 至 50% 投票權
             Investments in associates are accounted for using                       的股權。於聯營公司的投資最
             the equity method of accounting (see (iii) below), after                初按成本確認，之後以權益會
             initially being recognised at cost.                                     計法入賬（見下文 (iii)）。

            accounting (Continued)
           (iii) Equity method                                               (iii) 權益法
                Under the equity method of accounting, the                      根據權益會計法，投資初步按
                investments are initially recognised at cost and                成本確認，其後進行調整以於
                adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share              損益確認本集團應佔被投資方
                of the post-acquisition profits or losses of the                收購後溢利或虧損，並於其他
                investee in profit or loss, and the Group’s share of            綜合收益或虧損確認本集團應
                movements in other comprehensive income or loss of              佔被投資方其他綜合收益或虧
                the investee in other comprehensive income or loss.             損的變動。已收或應收聯營公
                Dividends received or receivable from associates are            司的股息確認為投資賬面值扣
                recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of             減項。
                the investment.

                Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-                 倘本集團應佔權益入賬投資的
                accounted investment equals or exceeds its interest             虧損等於或超過其於該實體的
                in the entity, including any other unsecured long-term          權 益（包 括 任 何 其 他 無 抵 押 長
                receivables, the Group does not recognise further               期 應 收 款 項），則 本 集 團 不 會
                losses, unless it has incurred obligations or made              確認進一步虧損，除非其已代
                payments on behalf of the other entity.                         表另一實體承擔責任或作出付
                                                                                款。

                Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group              本集團與其聯營公司之間交易
                and its associates are eliminated to the extent of the          的未變現利得按本集團於該等
                Group’s interest in these entities. Unrealised losses           實體的權益予以對銷。未變現
                are also eliminated unless the transaction provides             虧損亦會予以對銷，除非該交

           Assets that are subject to amortisation are reviewed for               當出現事件或情況改變顯示未必能

           indicate that the carrying amount may not be recoverable.              進行減值檢討。減值虧損按資產賬

           the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable                    可收回金額指資產公平值減出售成
           amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s             本與使用價值間的較高者。就減值
           fair value less costs of disposal and value in use. For the            評估而言，資產按可獨立分辨現金
           purposes of assessing impairment, assets are grouped at                流量的最低水平（現金產生單位）分
           the lowest levels for which there are separately identifiable          類。商譽以外的已減值非金融資產，
           cash flows (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets               於各報告期末檢討是否可能撥回減
           other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are                    值。
           reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at the end
           of each reporting period.

   39.9 Offsetting financial instruments                                   39.9 抵銷金融工具
       Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount        當有法定可執行權力可抵銷已確認
       is reported in the consolidated balance sheet when there is           金額，並有意圖按淨額基準結算或
       a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised amounts          同時變現資產及結算負債時，金融
       and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or realise         資產與負債可互相抵銷，並在合併
       the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The Group          資產負債表報告其淨額。本集團亦
       has also entered into arrangements that do not meet the               已訂立並不符合抵銷標準的安排，
       criteria for offsetting but still allow for the related amounts       但仍允許相關金額在如破產或終止
       to be set off in certain circumstances, such as bankruptcy            合約的若干情況下予以抵銷。
       or the termination of a contract.

       The Group has no financial assets and liabilities that need           於二零二五年及二零二四年十二月
       to be offset as at 31 December 2025 and 2024.                         三十一日，本集團並無金融資產及
                                                                             負債需要抵銷。

       over the period of the borrowings using the effective                  倘很有可能提取部分或全部融資，

       that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be           本化以作為流動資金服務的預付款

       liquidity services and amortised over the period of the
       facility to which it relates.

       39.18 Current and deferred income tax                                  39.18 即期及遞延所得稅
           The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax            期內所得稅開支或抵免指根據各司
           payable on the current period’s taxable income based on               法權區的適用所得稅率按即期應課
           the applicable income tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted         稅收入支付的稅項，並根據由於暫
           by changes in deferred income tax assets and liabilities              時性差額及未使用稅務虧損而導致
           attributable to temporary differences and to unused tax               的遞延所得稅資產和負債變動作出
           losses.                                                               調整。

           (a)   Current income tax                                              (a)   即期所得稅
                 The current income tax charge is calculated on                        即期所得稅支出根據本公司及
                 the basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively                    其附屬公司營運及產生應課稅
                 enacted at the balance sheet date in the countries                    收入所在國家於資產負債表日
                 where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and                    已頒佈或實質頒佈的稅務法例
                 generate taxable income. Management periodically                      計算。管理層就適用稅務條例
                 evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to              以詮釋為準的情況定期評估稅

             Deferred income tax is recognised, using the liability                 遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產
             method, on temporary differences arising between the                   及負債的稅基與資產及負債在
             tax bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying                 合併財務報表內之賬面值產生
             amounts in the consolidated financial statements.                      的暫時性差額進行確認。然而，
             However, the deferred income tax is not accounted                      倘遞延所得稅乃因資產或負債
             for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset                  在一宗交易（業務合併除外）中
             or liability in a transaction other than a business                    獲初步確認而產生，並且於交
             combination that at the time of the transaction affects                易時對會計或應課稅溢利或虧
             neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred                損均無影響，則遞延所得稅不
             income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws)                    計算入賬。遞延所得稅採用在
             that have been enacted or substantially enacted by                     資產負債表日已頒佈或實質頒
             the balance sheet date and are expected to apply                       佈之稅率（及法例）釐定，並預
             when the related deferred income tax asset is realised                 期在變現有關遞延所得稅資產
             or the deferred income tax liability is settled.                       或償付遞延所得稅負債時將會
                                                                                    適用。

             Deferred income tax assets are recognised only if it is                遞延所得稅資產僅在很有可能
             probable that future taxable amounts will be available                 將未來應課稅款項與可動用之
             to utilise those temporary differences and losses.                     暫時性差額及虧損抵銷情況下
                                                                                    確認。

             Deferred income tax liabilities and assets are not                     倘本公司可控制暫時性差額撥
             recognised for temporary differences between the                       回的時間，且該差額於可預見
             carrying amount and tax bases of investments in                        的將來很可能不會撥回，則不
             foreign operations where the company is able to                        會就賬面值與海外業務投資之
             control the timing of the reversal of the temporary                    稅基之間的暫時性差額確認遞
             differences and it is probable that the differences will               延所得稅負債及資產。
             not reverse in the foreseeable future.

             Deferred income tax assets and liabilities are offset                  在有合法可執行權利抵銷即期
             when there is a legally enforceable right to offset                    所得稅資產及負債時，以及在
             current income tax assets and liabilities and when                     遞延所得稅結餘涉及同一稅務
             the deferred income tax balances relate to the same                    機關時，方可抵銷遞延所得稅
             taxation authority. Current income tax assets and                      資產及負債。在實體擁有合法
             tax liabilities are offset where the entity has a legally              可執行權利以抵銷並擬以淨額
             enforceable right to offset and intends either to settle               基準結算或變現資產並同時結
             on a net basis, or to realise the asset and settle the                 清負債時，方可抵銷即期所得
             liability simultaneously.                                              稅資產及負債。

       39.23 Leases                                                           39.23 租賃
           Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                   在本集團可使用租賃資產之日，租
           corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset         賃確認為使用權資產及相應負債。
           is available for use by the Group.

           Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially             租賃產生的資產及負債按現值基準
           measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities                  進行初始計量。租賃負債包括固定
           include the net present value of fixed payments (including            付款額（包括實質固定付款額）扣除
           in-substance fixed payments), less any lease incentives               任何應收的租賃激勵的淨現值。
           receivable.

           The lease payments are discounted using the interest                  租賃付款使用租賃中內含的利率進
           rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily            行貼現。倘無法輕易確定該利率（為
           determined, which is generally the case for leases in the             本 集 團 租 賃 的 一 般 情 況），則 使 用
           Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,               承租人的增量借款利率，即該承租
           being the rate that the lessee would have to pay to borrow            人在類似經濟環境中按類似條款及
           the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in            條件借入獲得與資產具有類似價值
           a similar economic environment with similar terms and                 的資產所需資金而必須支付的利率。
           conditions.

  is responsible for financial management, capital markets, corporate   關係事宜，並協助本集團制定戰略規劃及長

  to develop strategic planning and long-term development plan.
